[Locus Award for Best Short Story](/wiki/Locus_Award_for_Best_Short_Story "Locus Award for Best Short Story")  
---  
1971-1979| 

  * "The Region Between" by [Harlan Ellison](/wiki/Harlan_Ellison "Harlan Ellison") (1971) 
  * "[The Queen of Air and Darkness](/wiki/The_Queen_of_Air_and_Darkness_\(novella\) "The Queen of Air and Darkness \(novella\)")" by [Poul Anderson](/wiki/Poul_Anderson "Poul Anderson") (1972) 
  * "[The Ones Who Walk Away from Omelas](/wiki/The_Ones_Who_Walk_Away_from_Omelas "The Ones Who Walk Away from Omelas")" by Ursula K. Le Guin (1973) 
  * "[The Deathbird](/wiki/The_Deathbird "The Deathbird")" by [Harlan Ellison](/wiki/Harlan_Ellison "Harlan Ellison") (1974) 
  * "[The Day Before the Revolution](/wiki/The_Day_Before_the_Revolution "The Day Before the Revolution")" by Ursula K. Le Guin (1975) 
  * "[Croatoan](/wiki/Croatoan_\(short_story\) "Croatoan \(short story\)")" by Harlan Ellison (1976) 
  * "Tricentennial" by [Joe Haldeman](/wiki/Joe_Haldeman "Joe Haldeman") (1977) 
  * "[Jeffty Is Five](/wiki/Jeffty_Is_Five "Jeffty Is Five")" by Harlan Ellison (1978) 
  * "Count the Clock that Tells the Time" by Harlan Ellison (1979)

  
1980-1989| 

  * "[The Way of Cross and Dragon](/wiki/The_Way_of_Cross_and_Dragon "The Way of Cross and Dragon")" by [George R. R. Martin](/wiki/George_R._R._Martin "George R. R. Martin") (1980) 
  * "[Grotto of the Dancing Deer](/wiki/Grotto_of_the_Dancing_Deer "Grotto of the Dancing Deer")" by [Clifford D. Simak](/wiki/Clifford_D._Simak "Clifford D. Simak") (1981) 
  * "The Pusher" by [John Varley](/wiki/John_Varley_\(author\) "John Varley \(author\)") (1982) 
  * "Sur" by Ursula K. Le Guin (1983) 
  * "Beyond the Dead Reef" by [James Tiptree Jr.](/wiki/James_Tiptree_Jr. "James Tiptree Jr.") (1984) 
  * "Salvador" by [Lucius Shepard](/wiki/Lucius_Shepard "Lucius Shepard") (1985) 
  * "With Virgil Oddum at the East Pole" by Harlan Ellison (1986) 
  * "[Robot Dreams](/wiki/Robot_Dreams_\(short_story\) "Robot Dreams \(short story\)")" by [Isaac Asimov](/wiki/Isaac_Asimov "Isaac Asimov") (1987) 
  * "Angel" by [Pat Cadigan](/wiki/Pat_Cadigan "Pat Cadigan") (1988) 
  * "Eidolons" by Harlan Ellison (1989)

  
1990-1999| 

  * "Lost Boys" by [Orson Scott Card](/wiki/Orson_Scott_Card "Orson Scott Card") (1990)
  * "[Bears Discover Fire](/wiki/Bears_Discover_Fire "Bears Discover Fire")" by [Terry Bisson](/wiki/Terry_Bisson "Terry Bisson") (1991) 
  * "Buffalo" by [John Kessel](/wiki/John_Kessel "John Kessel") (1992) 
  * "[Even the Queen](/wiki/Even_the_Queen "Even the Queen")" by Connie Willis (1993) 
  * "Close Encounter" by Connie Willis (1994) 
  * "[None So Blind](/wiki/None_So_Blind_\(short_story\) "None So Blind \(short story\)")" by Joe Haldeman (1995) 
  * "[The Lincoln Train](/wiki/The_Lincoln_Train "The Lincoln Train")" by [Maureen F. McHugh](/wiki/Maureen_F._McHugh "Maureen F. McHugh") (1996) 
  * "Gone" by [John Crowley](/wiki/John_Crowley_\(author\) "John Crowley \(author\)") (1997) 
  * "Itsy Bitsy Spider" by [James Patrick Kelly](/wiki/James_Patrick_Kelly "James Patrick Kelly") (1998) 
  * "Maneki Neko" by [Bruce Sterling](/wiki/Bruce_Sterling "Bruce Sterling") (1999)

  
2000-2009| 

  * "[macs](/wiki/Macs_\(short_story\) "Macs \(short story\)")" by [Terry Bisson](/wiki/Terry_Bisson "Terry Bisson") (2000) 
  * "The Missing Mass" by [Larry Niven](/wiki/Larry_Niven "Larry Niven") (2001) 
  * "The Bones of the Earth" by Ursula K. Le Guin (2002) 
  * "October in the Chair" by [Neil Gaiman](/wiki/Neil_Gaiman "Neil Gaiman") (2003) 
  * "[Closing Time](/wiki/Fragile_Things "Fragile Things")" by [Neil Gaiman](/wiki/Neil_Gaiman "Neil Gaiman") (2004) 
  * "Forbidden Brides of the Faceless Slaves in the Nameless House of the Night of Dread Desire" by [Neil Gaiman](/wiki/Neil_Gaiman "Neil Gaiman") (2005) 
  * "Sunbird" by [Neil Gaiman](/wiki/Neil_Gaiman "Neil Gaiman") (2006) 
  * "[How to Talk to Girls at Parties](/wiki/How_to_Talk_to_Girls_at_Parties "How to Talk to Girls at Parties")" by [Neil Gaiman](/wiki/Neil_Gaiman "Neil Gaiman") (2007) 
  * "A Small Room in Koboldtown" by [Michael Swanwick](/wiki/Michael_Swanwick "Michael Swanwick") (2008) 
  * "[Exhalation](/wiki/Exhalation_\(short_story\) "Exhalation \(short story\)")" by [Ted Chiang](/wiki/Ted_Chiang "Ted Chiang") (2009)

  
2010-2019| 

  * "An Invocation of Incuriosity" by [Neil Gaiman](/wiki/Neil_Gaiman "Neil Gaiman") (2010) 
  * "The Thing About Cassandra" by [Neil Gaiman](/wiki/Neil_Gaiman "Neil Gaiman") (2011) 
  * "The Case of Death and Honey" by [Neil Gaiman](/wiki/Neil_Gaiman "Neil Gaiman") (2012) 
  * "Immersion" by [Aliette de Bodard](/wiki/Aliette_de_Bodard "Aliette de Bodard") (2013) 
  * "[The Road of Needles](/wiki/The_Road_of_Needles "The Road of Needles")" by [Caitlín R. Kiernan](/wiki/Caitl%C3%ADn_R._Kiernan "Caitlín R. Kiernan") (2014) 
  * "The Truth About Owls" by [Amal El-Mohtar](/wiki/Amal_El-Mohtar "Amal El-Mohtar") (2015) 
  * "[Cat Pictures Please](/wiki/Cat_Pictures_Please "Cat Pictures Please")" by [Naomi Kritzer](/wiki/Naomi_Kritzer "Naomi Kritzer") (2016)
  * "[Seasons of Glass and Iron](/wiki/Seasons_of_Glass_and_Iron "Seasons of Glass and Iron")" by [Amal El-Mohtar](/wiki/Amal_El-Mohtar "Amal El-Mohtar") (2017)
  * "The Martian Obelisk" by [Linda Nagata](/wiki/Linda_Nagata "Linda Nagata") (2018)
  * "[The Secret Lives of the Nine Negro Teeth of George Washington](/wiki/The_Secret_Lives_of_the_Nine_Negro_Teeth_of_George_Washington "The Secret Lives of the Nine Negro Teeth of George Washington")" by [P. Djèlí Clark](/wiki/P._Dj%C3%A8l%C3%AD_Clark "P. Djèlí Clark") (2019)

  
2020-present| 

  * "The Bookstore at the End of America" by [Charlie Jane Anders](/wiki/Charlie_Jane_Anders "Charlie Jane Anders") (2020)
  * "Little Free Library" by [Naomi Kritzer](/wiki/Naomi_Kritzer "Naomi Kritzer") (2021)
  * "[Where Oaken Hearts Do Gather](/wiki/Where_Oaken_Hearts_Do_Gather "Where Oaken Hearts Do Gather")" by [Sarah Pinsker](/wiki/Sarah_Pinsker "Sarah Pinsker") (2022)
  * "[Rabbit Test](/wiki/Rabbit_Test_\(short_story\) "Rabbit Test \(short story\)")" by [Samantha Mills](/wiki/Samantha_Mills_\(author\) "Samantha Mills \(author\)") (2023)
  * "How to Raise a Kraken in Your Bathtub" by [P. Djèlí Clark](/wiki/P._Dj%C3%A8l%C3%AD_Clark "P. Djèlí Clark") (2024)

  *[POTUS  
No.]: Number
  *[b.]: born
  *[q.v.]: see also
  *[25]: Not actually given in Africanus; the number is deduced from Manetho's other copyists, as well as Africanus' own sum of years.
  *[69 years]: The correct sum should be 97; the total excluding Queen Hatshepsut is 75 years.
  *[263 years]: This is actually the sum of the first 13 kings, which amounts to 262 years. Adding the reign of the 14th king raises the number to 267 years.
  *[348 years]: The sum of the first thirteen kings is 263 years, just as Africanus. The "correct" sum of years would be 376.
  *[41 years]: Africanus gives 46 years, but this does not match the stated total.
  *[35 years]: Africanus gives 14 years, but this does not match the stated total.
  *[ 120 years]: Africanus' sum of items comes to 130 years.
  *[40 years]: Eusebius gives 25 years
  *[8 years]: Eusebius gives 12 years
  *[ 40 years]: Eusebius gives 44 years
  *[14 years]: Eusebius gives 12 years
  *[18 years]: Eusebius gives 20 years
